### 6. Apparatus problems (27 items)

*Index and appendix references that do not resolve, footnote numbering that broke, cross-references to
folios that do not exist, and the folio-marker error that governs every citation in chunks 016–019.
A26 is the one that needs a repair to the transcription rather than a reading.*

**A1. Two contents entries share a printed page that the sequence forbids.** Entries nos. 189 and 190
are both given the printed page "**۳۳۴**" where the surrounding sequence (۲۳۳ then ۲۳۵) requires
**۲۳۴** (p. 20, folio 8; chunk 001). Unflagged by the transcriber.

**A2. A contents column is headed wrongly on one folio.** The third column on folio 5 is headed
"**نمبر شمار**" rather than "**صفحہ**" as on every other contents page (p. 17, folio 5; chunk 001).

**A3. Two contents serial numbers contradict their own sequence.** "۳۴ [OCR?]" where 32 is expected
(printed p. 61) and "۲۵ [OCR?]" where 35 is expected (printed p. 64) (pp. 15–16, folios 3–4; chunk
001). See N4.

**A4. A page range that cannot be right as printed.** "ص **۳۵م [OCR?]** — ۴۴۷" in حاشیہ 1, "which
cannot be a correct range as printed" (p. 23, folio 11; chunk 001).

**A5. Two Storey citations that cannot both be right for the same volume and part.**
"ص ۹۲۳ - ۱۳۴۷ [OCR?]" and "pp 1044 [OCR?]" (pp. 30–31, folios 18–19; chunk 002).

**A6. One page reference given for two different quotations.** "ص ۵۵ [OCR?]" is given for two
different quotations from *Bahjat al-Asrar* (pp. 39–40, folios 27–28; chunk 003).

**A7. A Quran citation that does not resolve.** The verse behind Shah Husain's turn is referenced
"**(قرآن ۳۲- انعام ۶)**", "which pairs a sura number with a differently named sura. **Carried over as
printed**" (p. 56, folio 44, حاشیہ 1; chunk 004).

**A8. Six reversed or truncated ranges.** "اخبار الاخیار ص **۵۲ - ۵۰**" (p. 89, folio 77 as printed;
chunk 008); "ص [OCR?] **۱۹۳-۴۳۰**" in the *Akhbar al-Akhyar* citation (p. 53, folio 41; chunk 004);
"بایزید بیات : تذکرہ ہمایوں و اکبر کلکتہ **۳۷۴ - ۳۰ [OCR?]**", "where the same work is cited as
'ص ۳۰' a page earlier" (p. 100, folio 88 as printed; chunk 009); the *Qiran al-Sa'dain* page run
"**۳۵۳۔۲۵۹ [OCR?]**", "reversed and flagged" (p. 213, folio 201; chunk 020); Asaf Jah I's dates
"**(۱۷۴۸ - ۱۶۷۱ء)**" — "the later year comes first; treat as 1671–1748 **and flag if quoted**"
(p. 116, folio 104, حاشیہ 2; chunk 010); and **`peer-makki`**'s "۶۱۲ھ/**۱۵۔۱۲۱۶ء**", whose "CE span is
printed in reversed order" (p. 350, folio 338; chunk 032).

**A9. A cross-reference that points to the wrong entry.** "ملاحظہ ہو ترجمہ نمبر **۶۷[OCR?]** کتاب
ہذا" is given for **Sayyid Atiq Allah Jalandhari**, "but entry no. 67 in this chunk is Shaikh Muhammad
Salim Lahori and Atiq Allah is entry no. **66**. The numeral is OCR-flagged; the second
cross-reference, '**ترجمہ نمبر ۸۷[OCR?]**' for Sayyid Alim Allah Jalandhari, is flagged too **and
cannot be checked from this chunk**" (pp. 113–114, folios 101–102, حاشیہ 2; chunk 010). See N33.

**A10. A cross-reference to an appendix page that settles two arguments and appears in neither.** "the
second appendix at '**صفحہ ۲۹۷**', invoked twice — for the Mujaddid's twenty-eight-link pedigree
(p. 130) and for Muhammad Abid's death year (p. 142). **Whatever it settles, it settles outside these
chunks**" (chunks 012, 013). Chunk 029 places folio 297 at p. 309, among the appendix items on the
Mujaddid's pedigree, Shah Sikandar Kaithali and Bahlol Gol — and **records no entry on Muhammad Abid
there**. Chunk 030 then finds an index line, "**فوق محمد الدین | ۲۹۷**", citing a folio **inside the
editor's own appendix**, where chunk 029 records no such name, with a near-twin line
"فوقی محمود دین | ۱۲۵" two lines below it: "**either the index covers the editor's back matter — which
would matter for the whole book — or the number, or the name, is corrupt. Neither is flagged**"
(p. 325, folio 313).

**A11. A printed page that does not identify a saint.** "Two entries are numbered 45 and 46 but **both
begin on p. 93**, and the table of contents gives **both printed p. 81** — consistent, not an error,
but it means **a citation to 'printed p. 81' does not identify which saint**" (p. 17, folio 5, and
p. 93; chunk 008).

**A12. One in-text footnote marker, four sources, no mapping between them.** At "۱۲۱۹ھ **(۱)**" the
footnote block "opens with an unnumbered sentence and then lists (۱)–(۴). **Which of the four supports
which part of the correction is not marked**, and the editor's own attribution runs through a third
party ('جو سید شرافت صاحب کے نزدیک درست ہے')" (p. 273, folio 261; chunk 026).

**A13. A footnote that may belong to either of two entries.** Footnote 2 on p. 239 "cites 'اعظمی ۱۱۳'
like footnote 1 (which is about no. 178, Ghaibi Shah), but its content — 'ہرچہ می گفت می شد', a tongue
like a sword, burial 'بیرون قلعہ در **ملکوٹہ [OCR?]**' — matches entry no. 179's… **Which Kashmiri
majzub lies 'outside the fort' cannot be settled from the chunk**" (pp. 239–240, folios 227–228; chunk
023).

**A14. A footnote block whose entries carry no markers.** "حاشیہ: ملاحظہ ہو : (۱) … (۲) … (۳)" follows
"(باقی برصفحہ آئندہ)" on p. 278 and "appears to continue Hamid Qari's note; **entries 246 and 247
carry no footnote markers, so the three sources should not be attached to them**" (p. 279, folio 267;
chunk 027).

**A15. A letter-number list with a repetition and an outlier.** "**۱۲۹**" appears twice, once flagged,
and "**۲۲۴ [OCR?]**" sits out of sequence among numbers in the 110s. "**Count the letters from the
*Maktubat* itself rather than from this list**" (p. 136, folio 124; chunk 012). See N39.

**A16. Entry numbers for the relic sections are the editor's, not the author's.** "۱۔ **(۲۰۲، ۲۰۳،
۲۰۴)**" is attached to material headed only "زیارات عالیات موجودہ شہر لاہور",
"تفصیل زیارات موجودہ قلعہ لاہور" and "تفصیل زیارات حصہ دوم", while the next numbered entry is ۲۰۵.
"**The inference that the three relic sections are entries 202–204 is the editor's numbering, not the
author's**, and implies chunks 023–024 carried entries 171–201. Verify against those chunks before
citing entry numbers for the relics" (p. 261, folio 249; chunk 025).

**A17. The bibliography's numbering is broken twice, and the item count assumes both are slips.** The
manuscripts run ۱۹ → "**۲۱[OCR?]**" with **no item ۲۰**; the English list runs "262: M. Latif:
Lahore." then "**: Friedman Yu : Shaykh Ahmad Sirhindi Canda. 1971.**" with **no number**. "**The
count of 265 items assumes both gaps are numbering slips rather than lost entries**" (pp. 293, 306;
folios 281, 294; chunk 028).

**A18. The appendix's page keys are not in sequence, and one is flagged.** Entry 83 at p. 117, entry 68
at p. 112, entry 92 at p. 130 — "but '**۱۲۹ ص ۹۴[OCR?]**' puts entry 129 at p. 94, **before** entries
68 and 83. **If entries run in page order, ۹۴ cannot be right**… **neither figure is usable**"
(p. 310, folio 298; chunk 029). See N96. Chunk 032 reports the same fault from the other end:
"**شمارہ، ۲۲ ص ۵**" stands first, before "شمارہ۔ ۱۱، ص ۳۸", where chunk 031 recorded the appendix's
earlier pointers as "شمارہ۔ ۷، ص ۳۴، شمارہ۔ ۲۳، ص ۵۱" — "so entry 22 should fall near printed page 50,
not page 5. **Both the numeral '۲۲' and the page '۵' are suspect; neither is flagged. Do not
correct**" (p. 344, folio 332).

**A19. Appendix and index disagree on Bahlol Gol's nisba and his page.** Appendix: "شمارہ ۶۸ ص **۱۱۲**
… شیخ بہلول گول **جالندھری**" (p. 309, folio 297). Index: "بہلول گول **برمکی** | **۱۰۲**" (p. 314,
folio 302). "'جالندھری' against 'برمکی', and 112 against 102. **One of the two numbers is a misprint or
a misread; the nisbas may both be his or one may be wrong.** Also a distinct 'بہلول قادری | ۳۷، ۳۸،
۴۲'" (chunk 029).

**A20. Appendix and index disagree on Shah Sikandar Kaithali's page.** The editor's correction is keyed
"**ص ۲۷**"; the index reads "سکندر کیتھلی شاہ | **۴۷** ، ۱۱۷", and "کمال کیتھلی شاہ | ۴۷ ، ۱۱۷"
repeats those two folios exactly. "**۲۷ and ۴۷ differ by one glyph and neither was flagged; the
grandfather/grandson pair sharing both folios favours 47. Flagged for a human, not decided here**"
(pp. 309, 319, 326; chunks 029, 030).

**A21. An index folio the same index omits for that saint's own entry.** Chunk 031 read
"مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت بخاری | ۱۵۳ ، ۱۵۵ ، ۱۵۷ ، ۱۵۸ ، ۱۶۱ ، ۱۶۹", "which **skips 156** — the page on
which, per [chunk 032], his own entry stands. **Either the index dropped a number or the annotation's
'ص ۱۵۶' is off by one. Flagged for a human**" (p. 330, folio 318; chunks 031, 032). Bears on
**`shrine-of-makhdoom-jahaniyan-jahangasht`**.

**A22. The last annotation's entry number contradicts the Third Appendix.** The final note is headed
"شمارہ۔ **۳۰۸**۔ ص ۲۶۰" while the Third Appendix points to
"کتاب ہذا مسلسل شمارہ ۔۔ **۲۴۰** ، صفحہ **۲۶۳ ۔ ۲۶۴**". "**Entry 240 cannot sit at printed pages
263–264 while entry 308 sits at page 260 if both series ascend.** Within this appendix the printed
pages do ascend monotonically (38, 65, 75, 92, 92, 97, 106, 111, 126, 151, 156, 164, 189, 260), so the
page 260 is likely sound and the serial '۳۰۸' is the suspect figure — **but it is unflagged and must be
checked in the PDF before either number is used**. Either way the book's entries and text run to at
least printed page 260" (pp. 351, 337; chunks 031, 032). See C2.

**A23. The index's page lists are not always ascending — a warning about every folio taken from it.**
"آدم بنوری | ۴۷، ۱۲۲، **۱۷۷، ۱۲۸**", "ابراہیم ادہم | **۷۵، ۷۲**",
"اسحاق گازرونی | **۱۹۱، ۱۷۹**", "اسماعیل محدث لاہوری | **۱۷۹، ۲۳۶، ۱۸۰**",
"اشرف جہانگیر سمنانی | **۱۴۷، ۱۲۹**، ۱۵۲…", "احمد کبیر | **۱۵۷، ۱۵۶**",
"بدر الدین سرہندی | **۱۲۳، ۴۸**" (pp. 311–314; chunk 029); and in the second index
"نور احمد چشتی | ۶۰ ، **۴۴ [OCR?]** ، ۵۳", "پنجاب | … ۱۴۴ ، **۱۰۰ [OCR?]** ، ۱۸۱",
"لاہور | … ۱۱۷ ، **۱۱۳** … ۱۴۷ ، **۱۶۰ [OCR?] ، ۱۶۰**", "وحدت عبد الاحد شاہ گل | **۱۹۲ ، ۱۲۰**"
(pp. 331–335; chunk 031). "**Either the printed index is disordered or digits have been transposed in
OCR; any single folio taken from this index should be confirmed on the page before it is cited**", and
"where a number breaks the ascending sequence it is probably misread; **use the neighbours**".

**A24. Structural defects in the two indexes.** Chunk 029, pp. 311–318: **an entry with the name lost**
— "**| ۱۸۵**" between "احمد تختہ" and "احمد سرہندی"; "احسن سید | ۷۳" printed twice;
"احمد خان سر سید" split into two lines; "ادھم کشمیری[OCR?] | ۱۹۲" out of alphabetical place;
"حسین مرزا سلطان (حاکم سندھ) |" **with no page**; "بی بی اولیاء | ۲۴۰" filed under ا;
"بہاری لاہوری خواجہ | ۵۳، ۵۴" after "بیج ناتھ" at the end of ب; **no ث heading**; ڈ and ذ sections of
one flagged line each. Chunk 030: **five lines print a name and the rule but no page number and no
cross-reference** — "شریف میر سید (والد حضرت ایشاں لاہوری) |" (p. 320), "عبد اللہ ثانی |" and
"عبد اللہ صومعی |" (p. 322), "علی شاہ رہبر کاکی |" (p. 324), "محمد ادریس لاہوری اوچی [OCR?] |"
(p. 327) — "so **a locator is lost either in the print or in the transcription**"; and alphabetical
order slips at several points, "so a name missing from its expected place may be a few lines away"
(pp. 319, 321, 323). Chunk 031: **eight place-index entries with no page number**, including
"**شیر گڈھ |**" — "whether the print is blank or the OCR dropped the numeral cannot be told; check the
PDF" (pp. 328, 333–335); **homonym entries the index does not resolve** — "محمد سید" four times with
different pages plus "محمد سید مفتی | ۱۶", "محمد علی | ۱۶۳" and "محمد علی | ۲۲۳",
"محمد سلیم لاہوری | ۲۰" and "محمد سلیم | ۸۴" — "**different men or a split entry — undeterminable
here**" (p. 328); and alphabetic misplacements that "may be the print's", "**a sign the index was
compiled by hand**" (pp. 329–333). Two further oddities: "**قرآن [OCR?] | ۱۷۰**" and
"**مانٹریال | ۱۷۰**" in a place index, "the first flagged and out of place; the second legible and
odd" (pp. 335–336; chunk 031); and **`ranmal-sharif`**'s page-list ends in a comma —
"نوشاہ گنج بخش (حاجی محمد) | ۵۸ ، ۵۹ ، ۶۰ ، ۶۴ ، ۶۵ ، ۶۸ ،" — "either a printer's slip or a lost
continuation. **Treat 68 as the last known page, not the last page**" (p. 332, folio 320; chunk 031).

**A25. The index breaks off mid-letter, and an absence there means nothing.** The last line of chunk
029's stretch is "سرخ بخاری اوچی …" (p. 318, folio 306); "the rest of س — **where Sakhi Sarwar,
Sulaiman Taunsvi, Shah Sulaiman Nuri and others would fall** — is in the next chunk. **Nothing in this
chunk should be read as the book's silence on those names.**" Chunk 030 then breaks off mid-م at
"محمد بن محمود | دیکھئے جلال الدین گازرونی" (p. 327), and chunk 031's index stretch is likewise a
partial view: "the ع section, where Ali Hujwiri (`data-darbar`) would be indexed, is not in this
chunk; **whether folio 182 is his entry is left open**" — a cluster of five names sits at folio 182
(ابوالفضل ختلی، ابوبکر شبلی، شیخ حصری، زید شہید، حسین اصغر) and the chunk cannot place it (pp. 311,
316, 318; chunk 029). Chunk 028 records the mirror-image caution for the editor's table: "**the table
is a tail. Row ۶۶ is the first on p. 291; rows ۱–۶۵ and the table's title are in an earlier chunk. Its
rows for archive saints that precede ۶۶ are therefore not here**" (p. 291).

**A26. The folio markers for pp. 176–197 are ten too high — nine chunks touched this and it needs a
repair, not a reading.** The relation **`folio = PDF index − 12`** holds from p. 14 to p. 352. The
proof is two impossibilities in the markers themselves: **p. 197 → "folio 195" is followed immediately
by p. 198 → "folio 186"**, a backward jump of nine in a sequential printed book; and **"folio 194" is
claimed for both p. 196 and p. 206**, two pages ten apart. The relation holds unbroken for pp. 168–175
(chunk 016) and again for pp. 198–204 (chunk 019) and pp. 205–352 (chunks 020–032), so **the markers
for pp. 176–197 are the error** (chunks 019, 020). Consequences to action:
- **The folio values recorded in chunks 016 (pp. 176–177), 017 (all) and 018 (all) are ten too high**,
  and chunk 017's paragraph asserting a genuine ten-page pagination jump "is mistaken" and was
  withdrawn by its own later note. Every folio in those three files needs ten subtracted from p. 176
  onward. "**A human should either re-mark the transcription for that stretch or record the correction
  once at book level**" (chunk 019).
- The run **begins inside chunk 006**: p. 76 is marked **[folio 74]** and p. 77 **[folio 75]** where 64
  and 65 are expected — "either the transcriber read ۶۴/۶۵ as ۷۴/۷۵ or the printed pagination jumps
  eleven pages"; the two last pages of that chunk "should be cited by PDF page, and the folio treated
  as unverified", and **entry no. 34 therefore spans two differently-numbered folios** — "do not read
  the gap as missing text" (chunk 006).
- Chunks 007 and 008 are **ten too high on every page**, each with independent checks against the
  contents (six in chunk 007, four in chunk 008): "**any citation taken from this chunk's markers as
  printed will point ten printed pages too far into the book**" (chunks 007, 008). Chunk 009 records
  where it ends: markers read ten too high on pp. 97–100 and **drop back to correct at p. 101**.
- Chunk 016's items for entries **114, 115 and 116** are written "folio 174/175 as printed" and
  "**must be resolved by a human against the PDF before [they enter] an entry**".
- Two places where the relation is silent rather than wrong: **p. 338 carries no folio marker** (a
  full-page plate; folio 326 on the relation, "not a break in the offset… but the page cannot be cited
  by folio", chunk 031), and **folio markers stop after p. 352**, pp. 353–361 carrying `[back matter]`
  or `[blank page]` (chunk 032). Front matter: pp. 1–13 carry no folio marker at all (chunk 001).
- **Every "est. p. N" in chunks 029–032 is arithmetic on this relation, not something the book
  states**, and the workers say so each time: "to be verified against the PDF before use".

reference that cannot be checked. Grouped so one pass over an image clears them with the numerals.

`hadeeqat_ul_aulia` — "record the printed form when citing the edition" (chunk 032). Also: **item ۱۱۴
is a different *Hadiqat al-Awliya*** (Abd al-Qadir Thattavi, 1016 AH, Sindh, p. 298), so "any
bibliography that lists this edition beside it needs both authors spelled out" (chunk 028). And two
absences at the end of the volume: "**there is no errata list, no separately headed list of sources or
manuscripts consulted, and no colophon by the author or editor — (not stated) for all three**"; the
book's text ends at p. 352 with "○○○" and nothing else (chunk 032).

---

*Two provenance cautions that apply to anything drawn from the apparatus rather than the text. The
appendix of present locations is **undated and its coverage rule is unknown** — compiled by
"میاں محمد دین کلیم" (p. 287, folio 275), no year, no statement of which entries it covers, and it
omits its own chunk's four Lahore entries and has no row for the Bibis: "**treat 'موجودہ محل وقوع' as
'present at an unstated date', not as current**" (chunk 027). And the editor's table's middle column
is **his reading of the 1875 text**, not the text itself: the heading
"پتہ بمطابق حدیقۃ الاولیاء ۱۸۷۵ء" says the column follows the book, "but the chunk contains none of
the author's entries to check it against. **Facts entered from the table should be attributed to 'the
1974 editor's table, reporting the 1875 text', not to the author directly**" (p. 291, folio 279;
chunk 028).*
